\displaystyle\leq\left(12+8\left(\kappa_{0}+2b+2d\right)\right)\left[\left(\left(C_{0}+\sqrt{C_{0}}\right)\eta+\left(C_{1}+\sqrt{C_{1}}\right)\widetilde{A}\right)\left(K\eta\right)^{2}+\left(C_{2}+\sqrt{C_{2}}\right)\Delta(K\eta)^{2}\right.

\displaystyle+\left.\left(C_{3}+\sqrt{C_{3}}\right)\mathcal{A}K^{2}\eta\right]

\displaystyle=:\left(\widetilde{C_{0}}^{2}\eta+\widetilde{C_{1}}^{2}\widetilde{A}+\widetilde{C_{2}}^{2}\Delta\right)\left(K\eta\right)^{2}+\widetilde{C_{3}}^{2}\mathcal{A}K^{2}\eta,

where the constants are defined as:

\displaystyle\widetilde{A}

\displaystyle=\max\left\{\sigma^{2},\sqrt{\sigma^{2}}\right\}

\displaystyle\mathcal{A}

\displaystyle=\max\left\{A,\sqrt{A}\right\}

\displaystyle\widetilde{C_{0}}^{2}

\displaystyle=\left(12+8\left(\kappa_{0}+2b+2d\right)\right)\left(C_{0}+\sqrt{C_{0}}\right)

\displaystyle\widetilde{C_{1}}^{2}

\displaystyle=\left(12+8\left(\kappa_{0}+2b+2d\right)\right)\left(C_{1}+\sqrt{C_{1}}\right)

\displaystyle\widetilde{C_{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle=\left(12+8\left(\kappa_{0}+2b+2d\right)\right)\left(C_{2}+\sqrt{C_{2}}\right)

\displaystyle\widetilde{C_{3}}^{2}

\displaystyle=\left(12+8\left(\kappa_{0}+2b+2d\right)\right)\left(C_{3}+\sqrt{C_{3}}\right).

From Proposition 9 in the paper
Raginsky et al. 2017
, we know that

\displaystyle\mathcal{W}_{2}(\hat{p}_{K\eta},p^{*})

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{2C_{LS}\left(\log\left\|p_{0}\right\|_{\infty}+\frac{d}{2}\log\frac{3\pi}{m}+\left(\frac{M\kappa_{0}}{3}+B\sqrt{\kappa_{0}}+G_{0}+\frac{b}{2}\log 3\right)\right)}e^{-K\eta/C_{LS}}

\displaystyle=:\widetilde{C_{4}}e^{-K\eta/C_{LS}}

Finally, we can have

\mathcal{W}_{2}(p_{K},p^{*})\leq\left(\widetilde{C_{0}}\sqrt{\eta}+\widetilde{C_{1}}\sqrt{A}+\widetilde{C_{2}}\sqrt{\Delta}\right)K\eta+\widetilde{C_{3}}\sqrt{\mathcal{A}}\sqrt{K^{2}\eta}+\widetilde{C_{4}}e^{-K\eta/C_{LS}}.

(74)

Too bound the
2
-Wasserstein distance, we need to set

\widetilde{C_{0}}K\eta^{5/4}=\frac{\epsilon}{2}\quad\mbox{and}\quad\widetilde{C_{3}}e^{-K\eta/C_{LS}}=\frac{\epsilon}{2}.

(75)

Solving the (
75
), we can have

K\eta=C_{LS}\log\left(\frac{2\widetilde{C_{3}}}{\epsilon}\right)\quad\mbox{and}\quad\eta=\frac{\epsilon^{4}}{16\widetilde{C_{0}}^{4}\left(K\eta\right)^{4}}.

Combining these two we can have

\eta=\frac{\epsilon^{4}}{16\widetilde{C_{0}}^{4}C_{LS}^{4}\log^{4}\left(\frac{2\widetilde{C_{3}}}{\epsilon}\right)}\quad\mbox{and}\quad K=\frac{16\widetilde{C_{0}}^{4}C_{LS}^{5}\log^{5}\left(\frac{2\widetilde{C_{3}}}{\epsilon}\right)}{\epsilon^{4}}.

Plugging
K
and
\eta
into (
74
) completes the proof.

Appendix F
Techinical Proofs

F.1
Proof of Lemma
12

Proof.

By the definition of
\xi
in (
E.4
)

\displaystyle\left\|\mathbb{E}\xi\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle=\left\|\mathbb{E}\tilde{g}({\mathbf{x}})-\mathbb{E}\nabla U({\mathbf{x}})\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle=\left\|\mathbb{E}\nabla U(Q_{w}({\mathbf{x}}))-\mathbb{E}\nabla U({\mathbf{x}})\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\nabla U(Q_{w}({\mathbf{x}}))-\nabla U({\mathbf{x}})\right\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq M^{2}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|Q_{w}({\mathbf{x}})-\nabla U({\mathbf{x}})\right\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq M\frac{\Delta^{2}d}{4}.

We also know that from the definition that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left\|\xi\right\|^{2}=\mathbb{E}\left\|\tilde{g}({\mathbf{x}})-\nabla U({\mathbf{x}})\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left\|Q_{G}(\nabla\tilde{U}(Q_{W}({\mathbf{x}})))-\nabla\tilde{U}(Q_{W}({\mathbf{x}}))+\nabla\tilde{U}(Q_{W}({\mathbf{x}}))-\nabla U(Q_{W}({\mathbf{x}}))+\nabla U(Q_{W}({\mathbf{x}}))-\nabla U({\mathbf{x}})\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left\|Q_{G}(\nabla\tilde{U}(Q_{W}({\mathbf{x}})))-\nabla\tilde{U}(Q_{W}({\mathbf{x}}))\right\|^{2}+\mathbb{E}\left\|\nabla\tilde{U}(Q_{W}({\mathbf{x}}))-\nabla U(Q_{W}({\mathbf{x}}))\right\|^{2}+\mathbb{E}\left\|\nabla U(Q_{W}({\mathbf{x}}))-\nabla U({\mathbf{x}})\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\Delta^{2}d}{4}+\sigma^{2}+M^{2}\mathbb{E}\left\|Q_{W}({\mathbf{x}})-{\mathbf{x}}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq(M^{2}+1)\frac{\Delta^{2}d}{4}+\sigma^{2},

where in the first inequality, we apply Assumptions
1
and
3
.

∎

F.2
Proof of Lemma
13

Proof.

Let
\Gamma_{1}
be the set of all couplings between
\widetilde{\Phi}_{\eta}q_{0}
and
q^{*}
and
\Gamma_{2}
be the set of all couplings between
\widehat{\Phi}_{\eta}q_{0}
adn
q^{*}
. Let
r_{1}
be the optimal coupling between
\widetilde{\Phi}_{\eta}q_{0}
and
q^{*}
, i.e.

\mathbb{E}_{(\theta,\phi)\sim r_{1}}[\left\|\theta-\phi\right\|^{2}]=\mathcal{W}_{2}^{2}(\widetilde{\Phi}_{\eta}q_{0},q^{*}).

Let
\left(\begin{bmatrix}\tilde{x}\\
\tilde{\omega}\end{bmatrix},\begin{bmatrix}x^{*}\\
\omega^{*}\end{bmatrix}\right)\sim r_{1}
. We define the random variable
\begin{bmatrix}x\\
\omega\end{bmatrix}
as

\begin{bmatrix}x\\
\omega\end{bmatrix}=\begin{bmatrix}\tilde{x}\\
\tilde{\omega}\end{bmatrix}+u\begin{bmatrix}\left(\int_{0}^{\eta}\left(\int_{0}^{r}e^{-\gamma(s-r)}ds\right)dr\right)\xi\\
\left(\int_{0}^{\eta}\left(\int_{0}^{r}e^{-\gamma(s-r)}ds\right)dr+\int_{0}^{\eta}e^{-\gamma(s-\eta)}ds\right)\xi\end{bmatrix}.

By equation (
44
),
\left(\begin{bmatrix}x\\
\omega\end{bmatrix},\begin{bmatrix}x^{*}\\
\omega^{*}\end{bmatrix}\right)
define a valid coupling between
\Phi_{\eta}q_{0}
and
q^{*}
. Now we can analyze the Wasserstein distance between
\Phi_{\eta}q_{0}
and
q^{*}
.

\displaystyle\mathcal{W}_{2}^{2}(\widehat{\Phi}_{\eta}q_{0},q^{*})

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}_{r_{1}}\left[\left\|\begin{bmatrix}\tilde{x}\\
\tilde{\omega}\end{bmatrix}+u\begin{bmatrix}\left(\int_{0}^{\eta}\left(\int_{0}^{r}e^{-\gamma(s-r)}ds\right)dr\right)\xi\\
\left(\int_{0}^{\eta}\left(\int_{0}^{r}e^{-\gamma(s-r)}ds\right)dr+\int_{0}^{\delta}e^{-\gamma(s-\eta)}ds\right)\xi\end{bmatrix}-\begin{bmatrix}x^{*}\\
\omega^{*}\end{bmatrix}\right\|^{2}\right]

(76)

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}_{r_{1}}\left[\left\|\begin{bmatrix}\tilde{x}-x^{*}\\
\tilde{\omega}-\omega^{*}\end{bmatrix}+u\begin{bmatrix}\left(\int_{0}^{\eta}\left(\int_{0}^{r}e^{-\gamma(s-r)}ds\right)dr\right)\mathbb{E}\xi\\
\left(\int_{0}^{\eta}\left(\int_{0}^{r}e^{-\gamma(s-r)}ds\right)dr+\int_{0}^{\delta}e^{-\gamma(s-\eta)}ds\right)\mathbb{E}\xi\end{bmatrix}\right\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle+\mathbb{E}_{r_{1}}\left[\left\|u\begin{bmatrix}\left(\int_{0}^{\eta}\left(\int_{0}^{r}e^{-\gamma(s-r)}ds\right)dr\right)(\xi-\mathbb{E}\xi)\\
\left(\int_{0}^{\eta}\left(\int_{0}^{r}e^{-\gamma(s-r)}ds\right)dr+\int_{0}^{\eta}e^{-\gamma(s-\eta)}ds\right)(\xi-\mathbb{E}\xi)\end{bmatrix}\right\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\left(\mathcal{W}_{2}(\widetilde{\Phi}_{\eta}q_{0},q^{*})+2u\sqrt{\eta^{4}/4+\eta^{2}}\left\|\mathbb{E}\xi\right\|\right)^{2}+4u^{2}(\eta^{4}/4+\eta^{2})\mathbb{E}_{r_{1}}\left[\left\|\xi-\mathbb{E}\xi\right\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\left(\mathcal{W}_{2}(\widetilde{\Phi}_{\eta}q_{0},q^{*})+\sqrt{5}/2u\eta\sqrt{d}M\Delta\right)^{2}+5u^{2}\eta^{2}\left((M^{2}+1)\frac{\Delta^{2}d}{4}+\sigma^{2}\right).

(77)

∎

F.3
Proof of Lemma
10

Proof.

In order to get the upper bound of
\left\|{\mathbf{x}}_{k}\right\|
and
\left\|{\mathbf{v}}_{k}\right\|
, we bound the Lyapunov function
\mathcal{E}({\mathbf{x}}_{k},{\mathbf{v}}_{k})
. By the smooth Assumption
1
, we know

U({\mathbf{x}}_{k+1})-U(x^{*})\leq U({\mathbf{x}}_{k})+\langle\nabla U({\mathbf{x}}_{k}),{\mathbf{x}}_{k+1}-{\mathbf{x}}_{k}\rangle+M^{2}/2\left\|{\mathbf{x}}_{k+1}-{\mathbf{x}}_{k}\right\|^{2}-U(x^{*}).

Recall the definition of the Lyapunov function

\mathcal{E}({\mathbf{x}}_{k+1},{\mathbf{v}}_{k+1})=\left\|{\mathbf{x}}_{k+1}\right\|^{2}+\left\|{\mathbf{x}}_{k+1}+2{\mathbf{v}}_{k+1}/\gamma\right\|^{2}+8u\left(U({\mathbf{x}}_{k+1})-U(x^{*})\right)/\gamma^{2}.

For the first two terms we have

\displaystyle\left\|{\mathbf{x}}_{k+1}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle=\left\|{\mathbf{x}}_{k}\right\|^{2}+2\langle{\mathbf{x}}_{k},{\mathbf{x}}_{k+1}-{\mathbf{x}}_{k}\rangle+\left\|{\mathbf{x}}_{k+1}-{\mathbf{x}}_{k}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\left\|{\mathbf{x}}_{k+1}+2{\mathbf{v}}_{k+1}/\gamma\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle=\left\|{\mathbf{x}}_{k}+2{\mathbf{v}}_{k}/\gamma\right\|^{2}+2\langle{\mathbf{x}}_{k}+2{\mathbf{v}}_{k}/\gamma,{\mathbf{x}}_{k+1}-{\mathbf{x}}_{k}+2({\mathbf{v}}_{k+1}-{\mathbf{v}}_{k})/\gamma\rangle

\displaystyle+\left\|{\mathbf{x}}_{k+1}-{\mathbf{x}}_{k}+2({\mathbf{v}}_{k+1}-{\mathbf{v}}_{k})/\gamma\right\|^{2}.

This implies the following:

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\mathcal{E}({\mathbf{x}}_{k+1},{\mathbf{v}}_{k+1})\right]

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}\left[\mathcal{E}({\mathbf{x}}_{k},{\mathbf{v}}_{k})\right]+4\mathbb{E}\left[\langle{\mathbf{x}}_{k},{\mathbf{x}}_{k+1}-{\mathbf{x}}_{k}\rangle\right]+\frac{4}{\gamma}\mathbb{E}\left[\langle{\mathbf{x}}_{k},{\mathbf{v}}_{k+1}-{\mathbf{v}}_{k}\rangle\right]+\frac{4}{\gamma}\mathbb{E}\left(\langle{\mathbf{v}}_{k},{\mathbf{x}}_{k+1}-{\mathbf{x}}_{k}\rangle\right)

(78)

\displaystyle+\frac{8}{\gamma^{2}}\mathbb{E}\left[\langle{\mathbf{v}}_{k},{\mathbf{v}}_{k+1}-{\mathbf{v}}_{k}\rangle\right]+\frac{8u}{\gamma^{2}}\mathbb{E}\left[\langle\nabla U({\mathbf{x}}_{k}),{\mathbf{x}}_{k+1}-{\mathbf{x}}_{k}\rangle+M/2\left\|{\mathbf{x}}_{k+1}-{\mathbf{x}}_{k}\right\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle+\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|{\mathbf{x}}_{k+1}-{\mathbf{x}}_{k}\right\|^{2}\right]+\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|{\mathbf{x}}_{k+1}-{\mathbf{x}}_{k}+2({\mathbf{v}}_{k+1}-{\mathbf{v}}_{k})/\gamma\right\|^{2}\right].

By the update rule in (
5
), we know that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\langle{\mathbf{x}}_{k},{\mathbf{x}}_{k+1}-{\mathbf{x}}_{k}\rangle\right]=\frac{1-e^{-\gamma\eta}}{\gamma}\mathbb{E}\left[\langle{\mathbf{x}}_{k},{\mathbf{v}}_{k}\rangle\right]+\frac{u(\gamma\eta+e^{-\gamma\eta}-1)}{\gamma^{2}}\mathbb{E}\left[\langle{\mathbf{x}}_{k},\tilde{g}({\mathbf{x}}_{k})\rangle\right],

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\langle{\mathbf{x}}_{k},{\mathbf{v}}_{k+1}-{\mathbf{v}}_{k}\rangle\right]=-(1-e^{-\gamma\eta})\mathbb{E}\left[\langle{\mathbf{x}}_{k},{\mathbf{v}}_{k}\rangle\right]-\frac{u(1-e^{-\gamma\eta})}{\gamma}\mathbb{E}\left[\langle{\mathbf{x}}_{k},\tilde{g}({\mathbf{x}}_{k})\rangle\right],

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\langle{\mathbf{v}}_{k},{\mathbf{x}}_{k+1}-{\mathbf{x}}_{k}\rangle\right]=\frac{1-e^{-\gamma\eta}}{\gamma}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|{\mathbf{v}}_{k}\right\|^{2}\right]+\frac{u(\gamma\eta+e^{-\gamma\eta}-1)}{\gamma^{2}}\mathbb{E}\left[\langle{\mathbf{v}}_{k},\tilde{g}({\mathbf{x}}_{k})\rangle\right],

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\langle{\mathbf{v}}_{k},{\mathbf{v}}_{k+1}-{\mathbf{v}}_{k}\rangle\right]=-(1-e^{-\gamma\eta})\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|{\mathbf{v}}_{k}\right\|^{2}\right]-\frac{u(1-e^{-\gamma\eta})}{\gamma}\mathbb{E}\left[\langle{\mathbf{v}}_{k},\tilde{g}({\mathbf{x}}_{k})\rangle\right].

Plug into the (
78
) yields:

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\mathcal{E}({\mathbf{x}}_{k+1},{\mathbf{v}}_{k+1})\right]

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}\left[\mathcal{E}({\mathbf{x}}_{k},{\mathbf{v}}_{k})\right]-\frac{4u(2-\gamma\eta-2e^{-\gamma\eta})}{\gamma^{2}}\mathbb{E}\left[\langle{\mathbf{x}}_{k},\tilde{g}({\mathbf{x}}_{k})\rangle\right]-\frac{4(1-e^{-\gamma\eta})}{\gamma^{2}}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|{\mathbf{v}}_{k}\right\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle+\frac{4u(\gamma\eta+e^{-\gamma\eta}-1)}{\gamma^{3}}\mathbb{E}\left[\langle{\mathbf{v}}_{k},\tilde{g}({\mathbf{x}}_{k})\rangle\right]+\frac{8u(1-e^{-\gamma\eta})}{\gamma^{3}}\mathbb{E}\left[\langle{\mathbf{v}}_{k},\nabla U({\mathbf{x}}_{k})-\tilde{g}({\mathbf{x}}_{k})\rangle\right]

\displaystyle+\frac{8u^{2}(\gamma\eta+e^{-\gamma\eta}-1)}{\gamma^{4}}\mathbb{E}\left[\langle\nabla U({\mathbf{x}}_{k}),\tilde{g}({\mathbf{x}}_{k})\rangle\right]+\left(\frac{4Mu}{\gamma^{2}}+3\right)\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|{\mathbf{x}}_{k+1}-{\mathbf{x}}_{k}\right\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle+\frac{8}{\gamma^{2}}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|{\mathbf{v}}_{k+1}-{\mathbf{v}}_{k}\right\|^{2}\right].

(79)

By Assumption
2
, we know that
\langle{\mathbf{x}}_{k},\nabla U({\mathbf{x}}_{k})\rangle\geq m_{2}\left\|{\mathbf{x}}_{k}\right\|^{2}-b
. We then assume
\eta\leq 1/(8\gamma)
and use the inequality
-x\leq e^{-x}-1\leq x^{2}/2-x
for any
x\geq 0
, it follows that

\displaystyle-\frac{4u(2-\gamma\eta-2e^{-\gamma\eta})}{\gamma^{2}}\mathbb{E}\left[\langle{\mathbf{x}}_{k},\tilde{g}({\mathbf{x}}_{k})\rangle\right]

\displaystyle=-\frac{4u(2-\gamma\eta-2e^{-\gamma\eta})}{\gamma^{2}}\left(\mathbb{E}\left[\langle{\mathbf{x}}_{k},\nabla U({\mathbf{x}}_{k})\rangle\right]+\mathbb{E}\left[\langle{\mathbf{x}}_{k},\tilde{g}({\mathbf{x}}_{k})-\nabla U({\mathbf{x}}_{k})\rangle\right]\right)

\displaystyle\leq-\frac{4u(2-\gamma\eta-2e^{-\gamma\eta})}{\gamma^{2}}\left(m_{2}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|{\mathbf{x}}_{k}\right\|^{2}\right]-b\right)+\frac{4u(2-\gamma\eta-2e^{-\gamma\eta})}{\gamma^{2}}\left(\frac{1}{8}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|{\mathbf{x}}_{k}\right\|^{2}\right]+2\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\tilde{g}({\mathbf{x}}_{k})-\nabla U({\mathbf{x}}_{k})\right\|^{2}\right]\right)

\displaystyle\leq-\frac{3m_{2}u\eta}{\gamma}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|{\mathbf{x}}_{k}\right\|^{2}\right]+\frac{4u\eta b}{\gamma}+\frac{8u\eta}{\gamma}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\tilde{g}({\mathbf{x}}_{k})-\nabla U({\mathbf{x}}_{k})\right\|^{2}\right],

where the first inequality is because of the Young’s inequaltiy and Assumption
1
and the last inequality is based on the inequality that
\gamma\eta-(\gamma\eta)^{2}\leq 2-\gamma\eta-2e^{-\gamma\eta}\leq\gamma\eta
. Again by Young’s inequality and the update rule in (
5
) we have:

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|{\mathbf{x}}_{k+1}-{\mathbf{x}}_{k}\right\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq 2\eta^{2}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|{\mathbf{v}}_{k}\right\|^{2}\right]+u^{2}\eta^{4}/2\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\tilde{g}({\mathbf{x}}_{k})\right\|^{2}\right]+\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\xi_{k}^{x}\right\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|{\mathbf{v}}_{k+1}-{\mathbf{v}}_{k}\right\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq 2\gamma^{2}\eta^{2}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|{\mathbf{v}}_{k}\right\|^{2}\right]+2u^{2}\eta^{2}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\tilde{g}({\mathbf{x}}_{k})\right\|^{2}\right]+\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\xi_{k}^{v}\right\|^{2}\right].

It is easy to verify the fact that
\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\xi_{k}^{v}\right\|^{2}\right]\leq 2\gamma ud\eta
and
\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\xi_{k}^{x}\right\|^{2}\right]\leq 2ud\eta^{2}
. Thus,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\mathcal{E}({\mathbf{x}}_{k+1},{\mathbf{v}}_{k+1})\right]

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}\left[\mathcal{E}({\mathbf{x}}_{k},{\mathbf{v}}_{k})\right]-\frac{3um\eta^{2}}{\gamma}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|{\mathbf{x}}_{k}\right\|^{2}\right]-\frac{3(1-e^{-\gamma\eta})-\eta^{2}(8Mu+u\gamma+22\gamma^{2})}{\gamma^{2}}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|{\mathbf{v}}_{k}\right\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle+\frac{36u^{2}\eta^{2}+2\gamma u\eta^{2}+\left(4Mu+3\gamma^{2}\right)\eta^{4}}{2\gamma^{2}}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\tilde{g}({\mathbf{x}}_{k})\right\|^{2}\right]+\frac{2u^{2}\eta^{2}}{\gamma^{2}}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\nabla U({\mathbf{x}}_{k})\right\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle+\frac{8u\eta(\gamma^{2}+2u)}{\gamma^{3}}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\nabla U({\mathbf{x}}_{k})-\tilde{g}({\mathbf{x}}_{k})\right\|^{2}\right]+\frac{(8Mu+6\gamma^{2})ud\eta^{2}+4(4d+b)u\gamma\eta}{\eta^{2}}.

If we set

\eta\leq\mbox{min}\left\{\frac{\gamma}{4\left(8Mu+u\gamma+22\gamma^{2}\right)},\sqrt{\frac{4u^{2}}{4Mu+3\gamma^{2}}},\frac{6\gamma bu}{\left(4Mu+3\gamma^{2}\right)d}\right\},

we can obtain the following,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\mathcal{E}({\mathbf{x}}_{k+1},{\mathbf{v}}_{k+1})\right]

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}\left[\mathcal{E}({\mathbf{x}}_{k},{\mathbf{v}}_{k})\right]-\frac{3um_{2}\eta}{\gamma}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|{\mathbf{x}}_{k}\right\|^{2}\right]-\frac{2\eta}{\gamma}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|{\mathbf{v}}_{k}\right\|^{2}\right]+\frac{(20u+\gamma)u\eta^{2}}{\gamma^{2}}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\tilde{g}({\mathbf{x}}_{k})\right\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle+\frac{2u^{2}\eta^{2}}{\gamma^{2}}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\nabla U({\mathbf{x}}_{k})\right\|^{2}\right]+\frac{8u\eta\left(\gamma^{2}+2u\right)}{\gamma^{3}}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\nabla U({\mathbf{x}}_{k})-\tilde{g}({\mathbf{x}}_{k})\right\|^{2}\right]+\frac{16(d+b)u\eta}{\gamma}.

(80)

Furthermore we can bound
\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\tilde{g}({\mathbf{x}}_{k})\right\|^{2}\right]
by the following analysis:

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\tilde{g}({\mathbf{x}}_{k})\right\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq 2\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\tilde{g}({\mathbf{x}}_{k})-\nabla U({\mathbf{x}}_{k})\right\|^{2}\right]+2\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\nabla U({\mathbf{x}}_{k})\right\|^{2}\right]

(81)

\displaystyle\leq 2\left((M^{2}+1)\frac{\Delta^{2}d}{4}+\sigma^{2}\right)+4M^{2}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|{\mathbf{x}}_{k}\right\|^{2}\right]+4G^{2},

where
G^{2}
is the bound of the gradient at
0
, i.e.
\left\|\nabla U(0)\right\|^{2}\leq G^{2}
. Thus we can have:

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\mathcal{E}({\mathbf{x}}_{k+1},{\mathbf{v}}_{k+1})\right]

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}\left[\mathcal{E}({\mathbf{x}}_{k},{\mathbf{v}}_{k})\right]-\frac{3um_{2}\eta}{\gamma}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|{\mathbf{x}}_{k}\right\|^{2}\right]-\frac{2\eta}{\gamma}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|{\mathbf{v}}_{k}\right\|^{2}\right]+\frac{(21u+\gamma)4M^{2}u\eta^{2}}{\gamma^{2}}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|{\mathbf{x}}_{k}\right\|^{2}\right]

(82)

\displaystyle+\left(\frac{2(20u+\gamma)u\eta^{2}}{\gamma^{2}}+\frac{8u\eta\left(\gamma^{2}+2u\right)}{\gamma^{3}}\right)\left((M^{2}+1)\frac{\Delta^{2}d}{4}+\sigma^{2}\right)

(83)

\displaystyle+\frac{(21u+\gamma)4u\eta^{2}}{\gamma^{2}}G^{2}+\frac{16(d+b)u\eta}{\gamma}.

(84)

If we set the stepsize

\eta\leq\mbox{min}\left\{\frac{\gamma m_{2}}{12(21u+\gamma)M^{2}},\frac{8(\gamma^{2}+2u)}{(20u+\gamma)\gamma}\right\},

then we have:

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\mathcal{E}({\mathbf{x}}_{k+1},{\mathbf{v}}_{k+1})\right]

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}\left[\mathcal{E}({\mathbf{x}}_{k},{\mathbf{v}}_{k})\right]-\frac{8um_{2}\eta}{3\gamma}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|{\mathbf{x}}_{k}\right\|^{2}\right]-\frac{2\eta}{\gamma}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|{\mathbf{v}}_{k}\right\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle+\left(\frac{16u\eta\left(\gamma^{2}+2u\right)}{\gamma^{3}}\right)\left((M^{2}+1)\frac{\Delta^{2}d}{4}+\sigma^{2}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{(21u+\gamma)4u\eta^{2}}{\gamma^{2}}G^{2}+\frac{16(d+b)u\eta}{\gamma}.

Furthermore by Young’s inequality and Assumption
1
, we can bound the Lyapunov function by the following:

\mathcal{E}(x,v)\leq 5/2\left\|x\right\|^{2}+\frac{12}{\gamma^{2}}+\frac{2uM}{\gamma^{2}}\left(3\left\|x\right\|^{2}+6\left\|x^{*}\right\|^{2}\right).

Then if
\gamma^{2}\leq 4Mu
, we have

\mathcal{E}(x,v)\leq\frac{16uM}{\gamma^{2}}\left\|x\right\|^{2}+\frac{12}{\gamma^{2}}\left\|v\right\|^{2}+\frac{12uM}{\gamma^{2}}\left\|x^{*}\right\|^{2}.

(85)

Thus,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\mathcal{E}({\mathbf{x}}_{k+1},{\mathbf{v}}_{k+1})\right]

\displaystyle\leq\left(1-\frac{\gamma m_{2}\eta}{6M}\right)\mathbb{E}\left[\mathcal{E}({\mathbf{x}}_{k},{\mathbf{v}}_{k})\right]+\left(\frac{16u\eta\left(\gamma^{2}+2u\right)}{\gamma^{3}}\right)\left((M^{2}+1)\frac{\Delta^{2}d}{4}+\sigma^{2}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{(21u+\gamma)4u\eta^{2}}{\gamma^{2}}G^{2}+\frac{16(d+b)u\eta}{\gamma}.

Finally we show that

\displaystyle\sup_{k\geq 0}\mathbb{E}\left[\mathcal{E}({\mathbf{x}}_{k},{\mathbf{v}}_{k})\right]

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}\left[\mathcal{E}(x_{0},v_{0})\right]+\frac{6M}{\gamma m_{2}\eta}\left(\frac{16u\eta\left(\gamma^{2}+2u\right)}{\gamma^{3}}\right)\left((M^{2}+1)\frac{\Delta^{2}d}{4}+\sigma^{2}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{6M}{\gamma m_{2}\eta}\frac{(21u+\gamma)4u\eta^{2}}{\gamma^{2}}G^{2}+\frac{6M}{\gamma m_{2}\eta}\frac{16(d+b)u\eta}{\gamma}

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}\left[\mathcal{E}(x_{0},v_{0})\right]+\frac{96u\left(\gamma^{2}+2u\right)}{m_{2}\gamma^{4}}\left((M^{2}+1)\frac{\Delta^{2}d}{4}+\sigma^{2}\right)+\frac{24(21u+\gamma)uM}{m_{2}\gamma^{3}}G^{2}+\frac{96(d+b)uM}{m_{2}\gamma^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\overline{\mathcal{E}}+C_{0}\left((M^{2}+1)\frac{\Delta^{2}d}{4}+\sigma^{2}\right),

(86)

where
\overline{\mathcal{E}}=\mathbb{E}\left[\mathcal{E}(x_{0},v_{0})\right]+\frac{24(21u+\gamma)uM}{m_{2}\gamma^{3}}G^{2}+\frac{96(d+b)uM}{m_{2}\gamma^{2}}
and
C_{0}=\frac{96u\left(\gamma^{2}+2u\right)}{m_{2}\gamma^{4}}
.
Moreover by the definition of Laypunov function, we know
\mathcal{E}(x,v)\geq\mbox{max}\{\left\|x\right\|^{2},2\left\|v/\gamma\right\|^{2}\}
. This further implies that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|{\mathbf{x}}_{k}\right\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\overline{\mathcal{E}}+C_{0}\left((M^{2}+1)\frac{\Delta^{2}d}{4}+\sigma^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|{\mathbf{v}}_{k}\right\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\gamma^{2}\overline{\mathcal{E}}/2+\gamma^{2}C_{0}/2\left((M^{2}+1)\frac{\Delta^{2}d}{4}+\sigma^{2}\right).

Combining with equation (
81
) we can bound
\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\tilde{g}({\mathbf{x}}_{k})\right\|^{2}\right]
as:

\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\tilde{g}({\mathbf{x}}_{k})\right\|^{2}\right]\leq 2\left((M^{2}+1)\frac{\Delta^{2}d}{4}+\sigma^{2}\right)+4M^{2}\overline{\mathcal{E}}+4G^{2}.

(87)

∎

F.4
Proof of Lemma
15

Proof.

By the update rule in (
1
), we have:

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|{\mathbf{x}}_{k+1}\right\|^{2}\right]=

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|{\mathbf{x}}_{k}-\eta\tilde{g}({\mathbf{x}}_{k})\right\|^{2}\right]+\sqrt{8\eta}\mathbb{E}\left[\langle{\mathbf{x}}_{k}-\eta\tilde{g}({\mathbf{x}}_{k}),\xi_{k+1}\rangle\right]+2\eta\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\xi_{k+1}\right\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|{\mathbf{x}}_{k}-\eta\tilde{g}({\mathbf{x}}_{k})\right\|^{2}\right]+2\eta d

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|{\mathbf{x}}_{k}-\eta\nabla U({\mathbf{x}}_{k})-\eta\left(\tilde{g}({\mathbf{x}}_{k})-\nabla U(Q_{W}({\mathbf{x}}_{k}))\right)-\eta\left(\nabla U(Q_{W}({\mathbf{x}}_{k}))-\nabla U({\mathbf{x}}_{k})\right)\right\|^{2}\right]+2\eta d

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|{\mathbf{x}}_{k}-\eta\nabla U({\mathbf{x}}_{k})-\eta\left(\nabla U(Q_{W}({\mathbf{x}}_{k}))-\nabla U({\mathbf{x}}_{k})\right)\right\|^{2}\right]+\eta^{2}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\tilde{g}({\mathbf{x}}_{k})-\nabla U(Q_{W}({\mathbf{x}}_{k}))\right\|^{2}\right]+2\eta d

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left(\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|{\mathbf{x}}_{k}-\eta\nabla U({\mathbf{x}}_{k})\right\|\right]+\eta\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\nabla U(Q_{W}({\mathbf{x}}_{k}))-\nabla U({\mathbf{x}}_{k})\right\|\right]\right)^{2}+\eta^{2}\left(\frac{\Delta^{2}d}{4}+\sigma^{2}\right)+2\eta d.

We know the fact that:

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|{\mathbf{x}}_{k}-\eta\nabla U({\mathbf{x}}_{k})\right\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|{\mathbf{x}}_{k}\right\|^{2}\right]-2\eta\mathbb{E}\left[\langle{\mathbf{x}}_{k},\nabla U({\mathbf{x}}_{k})\rangle\right]+\eta^{2}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\nabla U({\mathbf{x}}_{k})\right\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|{\mathbf{x}}_{k}\right\|^{2}\right]+2\eta\left(b-m_{2}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|{\mathbf{x}}_{k}\right\|^{2}\right]\right)+2\eta^{2}\left(M^{2}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|{\mathbf{x}}_{k}\right\|^{2}\right]+G^{2}\right)

\displaystyle=\left(1-2\eta m_{2}+2\eta^{2}M^{2}\right)\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|{\mathbf{x}}_{k}\right\|^{2}\right]+2\eta b+2\eta^{2}G^{2}.

For any
\eta\in\left(0,1\wedge\frac{m_{2}}{2M^{2}}\right)
, if
0<1-2\eta m_{2}+2\eta^{2}M^{2}<1
and set
c=\frac{\eta m_{2}-\eta^{2}M^{2}}{1-2\eta m+2\eta^{2}M^{2}}
, then we have:

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|{\mathbf{x}}_{k+1}\right\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\left(1+c\right)\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|{\mathbf{x}}_{k}-\eta\nabla U({\mathbf{x}}_{k})\right\|^{2}\right]+\left(1+\frac{1}{c}\right)\eta^{2}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\nabla U(Q_{W}({\mathbf{x}}_{k}))-\nabla U({\mathbf{x}}_{k})\right\|^{2}\right]+\eta^{2}\left(\frac{\Delta^{2}d}{4}+\sigma^{2}\right)+2\eta d

(88)

\displaystyle\leq\left(1-\eta m_{2}+\eta^{2}M^{2}\right)\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|{\mathbf{x}}_{k}\right\|^{2}\right]+\frac{1-\eta m_{2}+\eta^{2}M}{\eta m_{2}-\eta^{2}M}\frac{M^{2}\eta^{2}\Delta^{2}d}{4}+\frac{1-\eta m_{2}+\eta^{2}M}{1-2\eta m_{2}+2\eta^{2}M^{2}}\left(2\eta b+2\eta^{2}G^{2}\right)

(89)

\displaystyle+\eta^{2}\left(\frac{\Delta^{2}d}{4}+\sigma^{2}\right)+2\eta d.

(90)

For any
k>0
we can bound the recursive equations as:

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|{\mathbf{x}}_{k}\right\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|x_{0}\right\|^{2}\right]+\frac{1-\eta m_{2}+\eta^{2}M^{2}}{\eta^{2}(m_{2}-\eta M^{2})^{2}}\frac{M^{2}\eta^{2}\Delta^{2}d}{4}+\frac{1-\eta m_{2}+\eta^{2}M^{2}}{\eta(1-2\eta m_{2}+2\eta^{2}M^{2})(m_{2}-\eta M^{2})}\left(2\eta b+2\eta^{2}G^{2}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{\eta(m_{2}-\eta M)}\left(\eta^{2}\frac{\Delta^{2}d}{4}+2\eta d\right)

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|x_{0}\right\|^{2}\right]+\frac{1-\eta m_{2}+\eta^{2}M^{2}}{\left(m_{2}-\eta M^{2}\right)^{2}}\frac{M^{2}\Delta^{2}d}{4}+\frac{1-\eta m_{2}+\eta^{2}M^{2}}{\left(1-2\eta m_{2}+2\eta^{2}M^{2}\right)(m_{2}-\eta M^{2})}\left(2b+2\eta G^{2}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{m_{2}-\eta M^{2}}\left(\eta\frac{\Delta^{2}d}{4}+\eta\sigma^{2}+2d\right)

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|x_{0}\right\|^{2}\right]+\frac{2M^{2}}{m_{2}}\frac{\Delta^{2}d}{4}+\frac{2}{m_{2}}\left(2b+2\eta G^{2}\right)+\frac{2}{m_{2}}\left(\eta\frac{\Delta^{2}d}{4}+\eta\sigma^{2}+2d\right).

Now if we let
\mathcal{E}=\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|x_{0}\right\|^{2}\right]+\frac{M}{m_{2}}\left(2b+2\eta G^{2}+2d\right)
, then we can write:

\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|{\mathbf{x}}_{k}\right\|^{2}\right]\leq\mathcal{E}+\frac{2\left(M^{2}+1\right)}{m_{2}}\frac{\Delta^{2}d}{4}+\frac{2\sigma^{2}}{m_{2}}.

∎

F.5
Proof of Lemma
11

Proof.

From the same analysis in (
80
), if we set

\eta\leq\mbox{min}\left\{\frac{\gamma}{4\left(8Mu+u\gamma+22\gamma^{2}\right)},\sqrt{\frac{4u^{2}}{4Mu+3\gamma^{2}}},\frac{6\gamma bu}{\left(4Mu+3\gamma^{2}\right)d}\right\},

we can obtain the following,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\mathcal{E}({\mathbf{x}}_{k+1},{\mathbf{v}}_{k+1})\right]

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}\left[\mathcal{E}({\mathbf{x}}_{k},{\mathbf{v}}_{k})\right]-\frac{3um_{2}\eta}{\gamma}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|{\mathbf{x}}_{k}\right\|^{2}\right]-\frac{2\eta}{\gamma}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|{\mathbf{v}}_{k}\right\|^{2}\right]+\frac{(20u+\gamma)u\eta^{2}}{\gamma^{2}}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|Q_{G}(\nabla\tilde{U}({\mathbf{x}}_{k}))\right\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle+\frac{2u^{2}\eta^{2}}{\gamma^{2}}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\nabla U({\mathbf{x}}_{k})\right\|^{2}\right]+\frac{8u\eta\left(\gamma^{2}+2u\right)}{\gamma^{3}}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\nabla U({\mathbf{x}}_{k})-Q_{G}(\nabla\tilde{U}({\mathbf{x}}_{k}))\right\|^{2}\right]+\frac{16(d+b)u\eta}{\gamma}.

(91)

By assumption
1
, we can bound
\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|Q_{G}(\nabla\tilde{U}({\mathbf{x}}_{k}))\right\|^{2}\right]
by the following,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|Q_{G}(\nabla U({\mathbf{x}}_{k}))\right\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|Q_{G}(\nabla\tilde{U}({\mathbf{x}}_{k}))-\nabla U({\mathbf{x}}_{k})+\nabla U({\mathbf{x}}_{k})-\nabla U(0)+\nabla U(0)\right\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|Q_{G}(\nabla\tilde{U}({\mathbf{x}}_{k}))-\nabla U({\mathbf{x}}_{k})\right\|^{2}\right]+2\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\nabla U({\mathbf{x}}_{k})-\nabla U(0)\right\|^{2}\right]+2\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\nabla U(0)\right\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\left(\frac{\Delta^{2}d}{4}+\sigma^{2}\right)+2M^{2}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|{\mathbf{x}}_{k}\right\|^{2}\right]+2G^{2}.

Plugging this bound into equation
(
91
)
, we can have:

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\mathcal{E}\left({\mathbf{x}}_{k+1},{\mathbf{v}}_{k+1}\right)\right]

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}\left[\mathcal{E}({\mathbf{x}}_{k},{\mathbf{v}}_{k})\right]-\frac{3um_{2}\eta}{\gamma}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|{\mathbf{x}}_{k}\right\|^{2}\right]-\frac{2\eta}{\gamma}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|{\mathbf{v}}_{k}\right\|^{2}\right]+\frac{2(20u+\gamma)u\eta^{2}M^{2}}{\gamma^{2}}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|{\mathbf{x}}_{k}\right\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle+\frac{(20u+\gamma)u\eta^{2}}{\gamma^{2}}\left(\frac{\Delta^{2}d}{4}+\sigma^{2}+2G^{2}\right)+\frac{2u^{2}\eta^{2}}{\gamma^{2}}\left(2M^{2}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|{\mathbf{x}}_{k}\right\|^{2}\right]+2G^{2}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{8u\eta\left(\gamma^{2}+2u\right)}{\gamma^{3}}\left(\frac{\Delta^{2}d}{4}+\sigma^{2}\right)+\frac{16\left(d+b\right)u\eta}{\gamma}

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}\left[\mathcal{E}({\mathbf{x}}_{k},{\mathbf{v}}_{k})\right]-\frac{3um_{2}\eta}{\gamma}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|{\mathbf{x}}_{k}\right\|^{2}\right]-\frac{2\eta}{\gamma}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|{\mathbf{v}}_{k}\right\|^{2}\right]+\frac{2(22u+\gamma)u\eta^{2}M^{2}}{\gamma^{2}}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|{\mathbf{x}}_{k}\right\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle+\frac{\left(20u+\gamma\right)\gamma u\eta^{2}+8\left(\gamma^{2}+2u\right)u\eta}{\gamma^{3}}\left(\frac{\Delta^{2}d}{4}+\sigma^{2}\right)+\frac{2(22u+\gamma)u\eta^{2}M^{2}}{\gamma^{2}}G^{2}+\frac{16\left(d+b\right)u\eta}{\gamma}

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}\left[\mathcal{E}({\mathbf{x}}_{k},{\mathbf{v}}_{k})\right]-\frac{3um_{2}\eta}{\gamma}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|{\mathbf{x}}_{k}\right\|^{2}\right]-\frac{2\eta}{\gamma}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|{\mathbf{v}}_{k}\right\|^{2}\right]+\frac{2(22u+\gamma)u\eta^{2}M^{2}}{\gamma^{2}}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|{\mathbf{x}}_{k}\right\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle+\frac{\left(36u+9\gamma^{2}\right)u\eta}{\gamma^{3}}\left(\frac{\Delta^{2}d}{4}+\sigma^{2}\right)+\frac{2(22u+\gamma)u\eta^{2}M^{2}}{\gamma^{2}}G^{2}+\frac{16\left(d+b\right)u\eta}{\gamma}.

If we set the step size
\eta\leq\frac{\gamma m_{2}}{6\left(22u+\gamma\right)M^{2}}
, we can have:

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\mathcal{E}\left({\mathbf{x}}_{k+1},{\mathbf{v}}_{k+1}\right)\right]

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}\left[\mathcal{E}({\mathbf{x}}_{k},{\mathbf{v}}_{k})\right]-\frac{8um_{2}\eta}{3\gamma}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|{\mathbf{x}}_{k}\right\|^{2}\right]-\frac{2\eta}{\gamma}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|{\mathbf{v}}_{k}\right\|^{2}\right]

(92)

\displaystyle+\frac{\left(36u+9\gamma^{2}\right)u\eta}{\gamma^{3}}\left(\frac{\Delta^{2}d}{4}+\sigma^{2}\right)+\frac{2(22u+\gamma)u\eta^{2}M^{2}}{\gamma^{2}}G^{2}+\frac{16\left(d+b\right)u\eta}{\gamma}.

(93)

Again from the same analysis in (
85
), if
\gamma^{2}\leq 4Mu
, we have

\mathcal{E}(x,v)\leq\frac{16uM}{\gamma^{2}}\left\|x\right\|^{2}+\frac{12}{\gamma^{2}}\left\|v\right\|^{2}+\frac{12uM}{\gamma^{2}}\left\|x^{*}\right\|^{2}.

Thus,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\mathcal{E}({\mathbf{x}}_{k+1},{\mathbf{v}}_{k+1})\right]

\displaystyle\leq\left(1-\frac{\gamma m_{2}\eta}{6M}\right)\mathbb{E}\left[\mathcal{E}({\mathbf{x}}_{k},{\mathbf{v}}_{k})\right]+\frac{\left(36u+9\gamma^{2}\right)u\eta}{\gamma^{3}}\left(\frac{\Delta^{2}d}{4}+\sigma^{2}\right)\

\displaystyle+\frac{2(22u+\gamma)u\eta^{2}M^{2}}{\gamma^{2}}G^{2}+\frac{16\left(d+b\right)u\eta}{\gamma}.

Finally, we show that for any
k>0
,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\mathcal{E}({\mathbf{x}}_{k},{\mathbf{v}}_{k})\right]

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}\left[\mathcal{E}(x_{0},v_{0})\right]+\frac{6M}{\gamma m_{2}\eta}\frac{\left(36u+9\gamma^{2}\right)u\eta}{\gamma^{3}}\left(\frac{\Delta^{2}d}{4}+\sigma^{2}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{6M}{\gamma m_{2}\eta}\frac{2(22u+\gamma)u\eta^{2}M^{2}}{\gamma^{2}}G^{2}+\frac{6M}{\gamma m_{2}\eta}\frac{16\left(d+b\right)u\eta}{\gamma}

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}\left[\mathcal{E}(x_{0},v_{0})\right]+\frac{54\left(4u+\gamma^{2}\right)u}{m_{2}\gamma^{4}}\left(\frac{\Delta^{2}d}{4}+\sigma^{2}\right)+\frac{12(22u+\gamma)uM^{3}}{m_{2}\gamma^{3}}G^{2}+\frac{96\left(d+b\right)uM}{m_{2}\gamma^{2}}

\displaystyle=:\mathcal{E}+C\Delta^{2}d.

Finally by the fact that
\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|{\mathbf{x}}_{k}\right\|^{2}\right]\leq\mathbb{E}\left[\mathcal{E}({\mathbf{x}}_{k},{\mathbf{v}}_{k})\right]
and
\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|{\mathbf{v}}_{k}\right\|^{2}\right]\leq\gamma^{2}\mathbb{E}\left[\mathcal{E}({\mathbf{x}}_{k},{\mathbf{v}}_{k})\right]/2
we can get our claim in Lemma
11
.

∎

Appendix H
Generalization of Theorem
4
under Relaxed Variance Assumption
7

All theorems presented in the paper assume bounded variance for the stochastic variance (i.e., Assumption
3
).
Another more flexible yet commonly used variance assumption
(
Raginsky et al. 2017
;
Gao et al. 2022
, e.g.,)
allows the variance scales with
\left\|{\mathbf{x}}\right\|^{2}
. In the rest of this supplementary material (Sections G-O), we generalize all the theorems in the main text to such a variance assumption, i.e. Assumption
7
.
Note that most of the arguments used in the proofs under Assumption
3
still hold, necessitating only slight modifications. Hence for the sake of readability, we only present the key changes due to the differences in the variance assumptions.

Assumption 7

(Bouned Variance)
.

There exists a constant
\tilde{B}\geq 0
, such that
\left\|\widetilde{\nabla U}(0)\right\|^{2}\leq\tilde{B}
. And there exists a constant
\delta\in[0,1)
such that:

\mathbb{E}{\left\|\widetilde{\nabla U}({\mathbf{x}})-\nabla U({\mathbf{x}})\right\|^{2}}\leq 2\delta\left(M^{2}\left\|{\mathbf{x}}\right\|^{2}+\tilde{B}^{2}\right),\quad\mbox{for any}\;{\mathbf{x}}\in\mathbb{R}^{d}.

Let Assumption
7
be true, we can then derive the following lemma.

Lemma 16
.

For any
{\mathbf{x}}\in\mathbb{R}^{d}
, the random noise
\xi
of the low-precision gradients defined in (
40
) satisfies:

\displaystyle\left\|\mathbb{E}\xi\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq M^{2}\frac{\Delta^{2}d}{4},

(94)

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[\left\|\xi\right\|^{2}]

\displaystyle\leq\left(2M^{2}+1\right)\frac{\Delta^{2}d}{4}+2\delta M^{2}\mathbb{E}\left\|{\mathbf{x}}\right\|^{2}+2\delta\tilde{B}^{2}.

(95)

Now, we are ready to present the Theorem
4
after revision.

Theorem 17
.

Suppose Assumptions
1
,
4
, and
7
hold and the minimum satisfies
\left\|{\mathbf{x}}^{*}\right\|^{2}<\mathcal{D}^{2}
. Furthermore, let
p^{*}
denote the target distribution of
{\mathbf{x}}
and
{\mathbf{v}}
. Given any sufficiently small
\epsilon
, if we set the step size to be

\eta=\min\left\{\frac{\epsilon\kappa_{1}^{-1}}{\sqrt{479232/5(d/m_{1}+\mathcal{D}^{2})}},\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{72\left(20u^{2}\delta M^{2}\kappa_{1}(d/m_{1}+\mathcal{D}^{2})+5u^{2}\left((2M^{2}+1)\frac{\Delta^{2}d}{4}\right)+\delta\tilde{B}^{2}\right)},\sqrt{\frac{e^{1/(4\kappa_{1})}-1}{10u\delta M^{2}}}\right\},

then after
K
steps starting with initial points
{\mathbf{x}}_{0}={\mathbf{v}}_{0}=0
,
the output
({\mathbf{x}}_{K},{\mathbf{v}}_{K})
of the SGHMCLP-F in (
5
) satisfies

\mathcal{W}_{2}(p({\mathbf{x}}_{K},{\mathbf{v}}_{K}),p^{*})\leq\tilde{\mathcal{O}}\left(\epsilon+\Delta\right),

for some
K
satisfying

K\leq\frac{\kappa_{1}}{\eta}\log\left(\frac{36\left(\frac{d}{m_{1}}+\mathcal{D}^{2}\right)}{\epsilon}\right)=\tilde{\mathcal{O}}\left(\sqrt{\delta}\epsilon^{-2}\log\left(\epsilon^{-1}\right)\Delta^{2}\right).

Proof.

For strongly log-concave target distributions, the equation (
77
) in Lemma
10
needs to be updated as the following,

\displaystyle\mathcal{W}_{2}^{2}(\widehat{\Phi}_{\eta}q_{0},q^{*})

\displaystyle\leq\left(\mathcal{W}_{2}(\widetilde{\Phi}_{\eta}q_{0},q^{*})+\sqrt{5}/2u\eta\sqrt{d}M\Delta\right)^{2}+5u^{2}\eta^{2}\left((2M^{2}+1)\frac{\Delta^{2}d}{4}+\delta M^{2}\mathbb{E}_{q_{0}}\left\|{\mathbf{x}}\right\|^{2}+\delta\delta\tilde{B}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\left(\mathcal{W}_{2}(\widetilde{\Phi}_{\eta}q_{0},q^{*})+\sqrt{5}/2u\eta\sqrt{d}M\Delta\right)^{2}+10u^{2}\eta^{2}\delta M^{2}\mathbb{E}_{\zeta}\left\|{\mathbf{x}}-{\mathbf{x}}^{\prime}\right\|^{2}+10u^{2}\eta^{2}\delta M^{2}\mathbb{E}_{p^{*}}\left\|{\mathbf{x}}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle+5u^{2}\eta^{2}\left((2M^{2}+1)\frac{\Delta^{2}d}{4}+\delta\delta\tilde{B}^{2}\right)

(96)

\displaystyle=\left(\mathcal{W}_{2}(\widetilde{\Phi}_{\eta}q_{0},q^{*})+\sqrt{5}/2u\eta\sqrt{d}M\Delta\right)^{2}+10u^{2}\eta^{2}\delta M^{2}\mathcal{W}_{2}^{2}(\widetilde{\Phi}_{\eta}q_{0},q^{*})+10u^{2}\eta^{2}\delta M^{2}\mathbb{E}_{p^{*}}\left\|{\mathbf{x}}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle+5u^{2}\eta^{2}\left((2M^{2}+1)\frac{\Delta^{2}d}{4}+\delta\delta\tilde{B}^{2}\right)

(97)

\displaystyle\leq\left(\left(1+10u^{2}\eta^{2}\delta M^{2}\right)\mathcal{W}_{2}(\widetilde{\Phi}_{\eta}q_{0},q^{*})+\sqrt{5}/2u\eta\sqrt{d}M\Delta\right)^{2}+10u^{2}\eta^{2}\delta M^{2}\mathbb{E}_{p^{*}}\left\|{\mathbf{x}}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle+5u^{2}\eta^{2}\left((2M^{2}+1)\frac{\Delta^{2}d}{4}+\delta\tilde{B}^{2}\right).

In the second inequality,
\zeta
is an optimal coupling between
q_{0}
and
q_{*}
.
{\mathbf{x}}
/
{\mathbf{x}}^{\prime}
are from the distributions
q_{0}
/
q^{*}
.

Then we choose
\eta\leq\sqrt{\frac{e^{1/(4\kappa_{1})}-1}{10u\delta M^{2}}}
, and following the same argument for deriving equation (
46
), we can have:

\displaystyle\mathcal{W}_{2}^{2}(\widehat{\Phi}_{\eta}q_{i},q^{*})

\displaystyle\leq\left(e^{-\eta/4\kappa_{1}}\mathcal{W}_{2}(q_{i},q^{*})+\eta^{2}\sqrt{\frac{8\mathcal{E}_{K}}{5}}+\sqrt{5}/2u\eta\sqrt{d}M\Delta\right)^{2}+10u^{2}\eta^{2}\delta M^{2}\mathbb{E}_{p^{*}}\left\|{\mathbf{x}}\right\|^{2}

(99)

\displaystyle+5u^{2}\eta^{2}\left((2M^{2}+1)\frac{\Delta^{2}d}{4}+\delta M^{2}B^{2}\right).

(100)

Moreover, we know the fact that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{p^{*}}\left\|{\mathbf{x}}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}_{p^{*}}\left\|{\mathbf{x}}-{\mathbf{x}}_{0}\right\|^{2}

(101)

\displaystyle\leq 2\mathbb{E}\left\|{\mathbf{x}}-{\mathbf{x}}^{*}\right\|^{2}+2\left\|{\mathbf{x}}^{*}-{\mathbf{x}}_{0}\right\|^{2}

(102)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2d}{m}+2\mathcal{D}^{2}

(103)

Thus plug it into the equation (
99
), we can have

\displaystyle\mathcal{W}_{2}(\widehat{\Phi}_{\eta}q_{i},q^{*})

\displaystyle\leq\left(e^{-\eta/4\kappa_{1}}\mathcal{W}_{2}(q_{i},q^{*})+\eta^{2}\sqrt{\frac{8\mathcal{E}_{K}}{5}}+\sqrt{5}/2u\eta\sqrt{d}M\Delta\right)^{2}+10u^{2}\eta^{2}\delta M^{2}\left(\frac{2d}{m}+2\mathcal{D}^{2}\right)

(104)

\displaystyle+5u^{2}\eta^{2}\left((2M^{2}+1)\frac{\Delta^{2}d}{4}+\delta\tilde{B}^{2}\right).

(105)

Finally, by invoking Lemma 7
Dalalyan & Karagulyan 2019
, we can have

\displaystyle\mathcal{W}_{2}(q_{K},q^{*})

\displaystyle\leq e^{-K\eta/4\kappa_{1}}\mathcal{W}_{2}^{2}\left(q_{0},q^{*}\right)+\frac{\eta^{2}\sqrt{\frac{8\mathcal{E}_{K}}{5}}+\frac{u\eta M\Delta\sqrt{5d}}{2}}{1-e^{-\eta/2\kappa_{1}}}

(106)

\displaystyle+\frac{10u^{2}\eta^{2}\delta M^{2}\left(\frac{2d}{m}+2\mathcal{D}^{2}\right)+5u^{2}\eta^{2}\left((2M^{2}+1)\frac{\Delta^{2}d}{4}+\delta\tilde{B}^{2}\right)}{\eta^{2}\sqrt{\frac{8\mathcal{E}_{K}}{5}}+\frac{u\eta M\Delta\sqrt{5d}}{2}+\sqrt{1-e^{-\eta/2\kappa_{1}}}\sqrt{10u^{2}\eta^{2}\delta M^{2}\left(\frac{2d}{m}+2\mathcal{D}^{2}\right)+5u^{2}\eta^{2}\left((2M^{2}+1)\frac{\Delta^{2}d}{4}+\delta\tilde{B}^{2}\right)}}.

(107)

Thus for some
K
satisfied

K\leq\frac{2\kappa_{1}}{\eta}\log\left(36\left(\frac{d}{m_{1}}+\mathcal{D}^{2}\right)\right),

we can get the following

\mathcal{W}_{2}(p_{K},p^{*})\leq\epsilon+\frac{16\kappa_{1}uM\Delta\sqrt{5d}}{2}.

(108)

∎

under Relaxed Variance Assumption

after revision.

Theorem 18
.

Suppose Assumptions
1
,
4
, and
7
hold and the minimum satisfies
\left\|{\mathbf{x}}^{*}\right\|^{2}<\mathcal{D}^{2}
. Furthermore, let
p^{*}
denote the target distribution of
{\mathbf{x}}
and
{\mathbf{v}}
. Given any sufficiently small
\epsilon
, if we set the step size to be

\eta=\min\left\{\frac{\epsilon\kappa_{1}^{-1}}{\sqrt{663552/5(d/m_{1}+\mathcal{D}^{2})}},\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{144\left(20u^{2}\delta M^{2}\kappa_{1}(d/m_{1}+\mathcal{D}^{2})+5u^{2}\left((2M^{2}+1)\frac{\Delta^{2}d}{4}\right)+\delta\tilde{B}^{2}\right)},\sqrt{\frac{e^{1/(4\kappa_{1})}-1}{10u\delta M^{2}}}\right\},

then after
K
steps starting with initial points
{\mathbf{x}}_{0}={\mathbf{v}}_{0}=0
,

for some

satisfying

. The revision is similar to the revision for Theorem
5
.

) need to be changed as the following:

\displaystyle+5u^{2}\eta^{2}\tilde{B}^{2}+5u^{2}\eta^{2}\frac{\Delta^{2}d}{4}+2u^{2}(A+B)

Appendix K
Generalization of Theorem
1
under Relaxed Variance Assumption
7

This section updates Theorem
1
’s proof, replacing Assumption
3
with
7
, and introduces the revised theorem.

Theorem 20
.

Assuming
1
,
2
,
7
, and
8
hold. Let
p^{*}
denote the target distribution of
({\mathbf{x}},{\mathbf{v}})
. If
\gamma^{2}\leq 4Mu
and setting the step size
\eta=\tilde{\mathcal{O}}\left(\frac{\mu^{*}\epsilon^{2}}{\log\left(1/\epsilon\right)}\right)
satisfying

\displaystyle\eta\leq\min\left\{\frac{\gamma}{4\left(8Mu+u\gamma+22\gamma^{2}\right)},\sqrt{\frac{4u^{2}}{4Mu+3\gamma^{2}}},\frac{6\gamma bu}{\left(4Mu+3\gamma^{2}\right)d},\frac{1}{8\gamma},\frac{\gamma m_{2}}{12(21u+\gamma)M^{2}},\frac{8(\gamma^{2}+2u)}{(20u+\gamma)\gamma},\frac{m_{2}\gamma^{2}}{12(\gamma^{2}+2u)M^{2}}\right\},

then after
K
steps starting at the initial point
{\mathbf{x}}_{0}={\mathbf{v}}_{0}=0
, the output
({\mathbf{x}}_{K},{\mathbf{v}}_{K})
of SGHMCLP-F in (
5
) satisfies

\mathcal{W}_{2}(p({\mathbf{x}}_{K},{\mathbf{v}}_{K}),p^{*})\leq\tilde{\mathcal{O}}\left(\epsilon+\widetilde{A}\sqrt{\log\left(\frac{1}{\epsilon}\right)}\right),

for some
K
satisfying

K=\tilde{\mathcal{O}}\left(\frac{1}{\epsilon^{2}{\mu^{*}}^{2}}\log^{2}\left(\frac{1}{\epsilon}\right)\right),

where constants are defined as:

\widetilde{A}=\max\left\{\sqrt{\Delta^{2}d}+\delta^{1/4},\sqrt[4]{\Delta^{2}d}+\delta^{1/4}\right\}
, and constant
1/\mu^{*}=exp\left(\mathcal{O}(d)\right)
denotes the contraction rate of underdamped Langevin dynamics (
10
).

Proof.

We first need to introduce a further assumption on the variance parameter
\delta
as the following assumption.

Assumption 8
.

Given Assumption
7
, we further assume the
\delta
satisfies the following condition:

\delta\leq\mbox{min}\left\{\frac{\gamma m_{2}}{12(21u+\gamma)M^{2}},\frac{8(\gamma^{2}+2u)}{(20u+\gamma)\gamma},\frac{m_{2}\gamma}{3(20u+\gamma)},\frac{m_{2}\gamma^{2}}{12(\gamma^{2}+2u)M^{2}}\right\}.

We need to revise the (
84
) as the following:

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\mathcal{E}({\mathbf{x}}_{k+1},{\mathbf{v}}_{k+1})\right]

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}\left[\mathcal{E}({\mathbf{x}}_{k},{\mathbf{v}}_{k})\right]-\frac{3um_{2}\eta}{\gamma}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|{\mathbf{x}}_{k}\right\|^{2}\right]-\frac{2\eta}{\gamma}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|{\mathbf{v}}_{k}\right\|^{2}\right]+\frac{(21u+\gamma)4M^{2}u\eta^{2}}{\gamma^{2}}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|{\mathbf{x}}_{k}\right\|^{2}\right]

(118)

\displaystyle+\left(\frac{2(20u+\gamma)u\eta^{2}}{\gamma^{2}}+\frac{8u\eta\left(\gamma^{2}+2u\right)}{\gamma^{3}}\right)\left((M^{2}+1)\frac{\Delta^{2}d}{4}+\delta M^{2}\mathbb{E}\left\|{\mathbf{x}}_{k}\right\|^{2}+\delta\tilde{B}^{2}\right)

(119)

\displaystyle+\frac{(21u+\gamma)4u\eta^{2}}{\gamma^{2}}G^{2}+\frac{16(d+b)u\eta}{\gamma}.

(120)

If we choose
\eta
satisfy the following condition

\delta\leq\eta\leq\mbox{min}\left\{\frac{\gamma m_{2}}{12(21u+\gamma)M^{2}},\frac{8(\gamma^{2}+2u)}{(20u+\gamma)\gamma},\frac{m_{2}\gamma}{3(20u+\gamma)},\frac{m_{2}\gamma^{2}}{12(\gamma^{2}+2u)M^{2}}\right\},

(121)

then we can have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\mathcal{E}({\mathbf{x}}_{k+1},{\mathbf{v}}_{k+1})\right]

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}\left[\mathcal{E}({\mathbf{x}}_{k},{\mathbf{v}}_{k})\right]-\frac{4um_{2}\eta}{3\gamma}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|{\mathbf{x}}_{k}\right\|^{2}\right]-\frac{2\eta}{\gamma}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|{\mathbf{v}}_{k}\right\|^{2}\right]

(122)

\displaystyle+\left(\frac{16u\eta\left(\gamma^{2}+2u\right)}{\gamma^{3}}\right)\left((M^{2}+1)\frac{\Delta^{2}d}{4}+\delta\tilde{B}^{2}\right)

(123)

\displaystyle+\frac{(21u+\gamma)4u\eta^{2}}{\gamma^{2}}G^{2}+\frac{16(d+b)u\eta}{\gamma}.

(124)

The remainder of the analysis is unchanged.

∎
